# Graph Report - autonomous-ai-agency  (2026-09-05)

## Corpus Check
- 1493 files · ~2,166,771 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 30226 nodes · 56488 edges · 1286 communities (1137 shown, 94 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 94% EXTRACTED · 6% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 3327 edges (avg confidence: 0.92)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `72e678da`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
